# Function names for the largest address-named functions

The build recipe (`build/units.json`), the link maps and the assembler key on the `FUN_<addr>` identifiers, so the live tree keeps them. This table is the rename map. `tools/apply_function_names.py <src-copy-dir>` rewrites a copy of `src/` (for the public export) with the new names; it never touches the build tree.

Sizes are bytes of the original function. Names come from referenced strings, named callees, enclosing class and file; functions with thin evidence were left alone.

| Address | Size | Old name | New name | Scope | Evidence |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 0x00741500 | 26620 | `FUN_00741500` | `DoIdle` | `CaravanGame` | Xbox 360 PDB name via fnv_decomp PR 42 (upstream name already matches; retail CaravanMenu::DoIdle; CaravanMenu.obj); was: UpdateGame -- CaravanGame member, 26 KB per-frame body; uses CGM_NPCTrackRect / CGM_PlayerTrackRect tiles (Caravan card game) |
| 0x00adf080 | 26108 | `FUN_00adf080` | `ProcessMessage` | `fnv_00adf080::BSAudioManager_adf080` | Xbox 360 PDB name via fnv_decomp PR 42 (string-set bridge; retail BSAudioManager::ProcessMessage; BSAudioManager.obj); was: ProcessSoundMessage -- BSAudioManager member taking SoundMessageRecord*; switches on GetType/GetArgument1/2 (audio message pump) |
| 0x00bf0720 | 24474 | `FUN_00bf0720` | `PresetADTSPasses_2x` | free function | Xbox 360 PDB name via fnv_decomp PR 42 (object-file gap fill; retail ShadowLightShader::PresetADTSPasses_2x; ShadowLightShader_Preset_2x.obj) |
| 0x0093e860 | 23042 | `FUN_0093e860` | `Update` | `TickPlayer` | TickPlayer member (float dt): per-frame player tick, UpdateTargetHealthHUD, physics-controller actor lookups |
